This creates a mismatch with modern structured agent workflows, in which independent branches explore subtasks, retrieve evidence, or generate candidate solutions before a final synthesis step. Existing systems typically merge these branches by concatenating their textual outputs, which discards the parallel structure and incurs redundant prefill computation. In this work, we introduce Parallel-Synthesis, a plug-and-play framework that enables a synthesizer to directly consume the KV caches produced by parallel worker agents. Parallel-Synthesis combines a cache mapper that calibrates independently generated branch caches with a fine-tuned synthesizer adapter that enables generation from this non-sequential cache interface. We train Parallel-Synthesis using data that exposes the synthesizer to parallel cache contexts, teaches aggregation across cached branches, and distills reasoning behavior from standard text-concatenation-based synthesis. Across nine downstream datasets spanning math, science QA, code generation, GAIA, and multi-agent database diagnosis, Parallel-Synthesis matches or outperforms text-based synthesis on seven datasets and remains close on the other two. It also reduces time-to-first-token by 2.5x-11x, suggesting that direct cache-based synthesis is a promising interface for more native and efficient synthesis over parallel agent branches.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [How Task Structure Limits Multi-Agent Success: An Information-Theoretic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13733)
*Shi Pan,Ming Luo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出多智能体系统在任务约束图存在高连通性且通信受限时，其成功率会受到信息瓶颈限制，并随最小割成本相关量呈指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统通常被认为可通过协作突破单智能体系统的能力上限，但实际效果并不总是随智能体数量或通信量增加而提升。论文试图从任务内在约束结构与信息传递限制出发，解释多智能体系统性能受限的根本原因。

Method: 作者建立信息论分析框架，将任务表示为潜在约束图，并考虑任务约束图在多个智能体之间划分时产生的信息瓶颈。定义最小割成本 C_min 来刻画跨智能体分区所需传递的关键信息量，并推导多智能体系统成功概率与该瓶颈之间的指数型关系。理论覆盖有外部反馈的开放系统与无外部反馈的闭环系统。

Result: 理论表明，在典型任务约束条件和有界通信条件下，多智能体系统成功概率与任务约束图连通性密切相关；当 C_min 较高时，成功概率会因跨分区信息瓶颈而指数下降。作者通过合成实验和 SWE-bench 提交数据对理论进行了验证。

Conclusion: 论文认为，有效的多智能体系统设计不能只依赖增加智能体数量或通信工程优化，而应显式考虑任务自身的约束结构。对于最小割成本较高的任务，更合理的策略是重构任务或重新设计分工方式，以降低跨智能体信息瓶颈。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) were expected to overcome the limitation of single-agent systems (SAS) through collaboration. However, under typicality conditions on the task's constraint graph and bounded inter-agent communication, we prove that the success probability of a MAS is closely tied to the connectivity of task constraints, where each agent has limited information-processing capacity. Specifically, the success probability decays exponentially with an information bottleneck that emerges from partitioning the task's constraint graph among agents. We define this quantity as the \emph{minimum cut cost} $C_{\min}$ of the potential constraint graph of each task. This information-theoretic bound applies to both open systems with external feedback and closed systems without. We validate our theory on both synthetic experiments and real-world empirical data from SWE-bench submissions. From our framework, effective MAS design should incorporate task-inherent constraints alongside engineering optimization, and when $\Cmin$ is high, practitioners should restructure tasks rather than simply scaling agents or communication.

</details>


### [156] [Non-Simple T-Prescriptions Yield T-Complexity Gains Infinitely Often](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13766)
*Thomas Schürmann*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明：对于任意固定的至少二元字母表，非简单 T-prescription 在无限多个最大码字长度 N 上，其 T-complexity 可严格超过所有最大长度不超过 N 的简单 T-prescription。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应 Titchener 等（2005）提出的问题：非简单 T-prescription 是否能在无限多个有限最大码字长度上取得比简单 T-prescription 更大的 T-complexity。

Method: 采用初等构造与阈值跳跃论证：由于已使用的 copy pattern 不能再次选择，简单 prescription 随步骤增加必须使用越来越多不同词，从而其最小长度阈值存在无限多次严格上升；在每个阈值跳跃处，将某个最小简单 prescription 的最后 copy factor 从 1 改为 2，构造有效的非简单 prescription。

Result: 构造得到的非简单 prescription 在保持精确最大码字长度 N 且低于下一简单阈值的同时，使 T-complexity 增加 log2(3)-1，因此在无限多个 N 上超过所有长度至多为 N 的简单 prescription 的最优值。

Conclusion: 该工作肯定并强化了原问题：不仅非简单 prescription 可在无限多长度上优于简单 prescription，而且非受限的精确长度最大值在无限多个 N 上严格大于相应的简单精确长度最大值。

Abstract: Titchener et al. (2005) asked whether non-simple T-prescriptions can achieve larger T-complexity than simple T-prescriptions for infinitely many finite values of the maximum codeword length. We answer this question affirmatively for every fixed alphabet of size at least two. In fact, we prove a stronger upper-envelope statement: for infinitely many lengths $N$, there exists a valid non-simple T-prescription of exact maximum codeword length $N$ whose T-complexity exceeds the best value attainable by any simple prescription with maximum codeword length at most $N$. Hence the unrestricted exact-length maximum over prescriptions is strictly larger than the corresponding simple exact-length maximum for infinitely many $N$. The proof is elementary. Once a copy pattern has been used, it cannot be selected again. Thus simple prescriptions with more and more steps must use ever more distinct words, which forces the minimal simple length thresholds to have infinitely many strict upward jumps. At each such jump, changing the last copy factor of a minimal simple prescription from $1$ to $2$ yields a valid non-simple prescription, thereby increasing T-complexity by $\log_2 3-1$ while staying below the next simple threshold.

</details>


### [157] [Practical Low-Weight Codes for Energy-Efficient Bus Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14203)
*Lorenzo Valentini,Marco Chiani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二进制消息连续传输时的写入/翻转开销，给出实用次优编码方案平均比特翻转数的闭式评估，并提出两种随机码本辅助的新方案；结果显示低复杂度方案接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非易失存储器和低功耗数据总线中，连续写入或传输数据时的比特翻转会影响器件寿命并增加能耗。传统写高效存储编码/总线编码可降低汉明距离，但最优方案复杂度较高，不利于能耗敏感和存储关键场景的实际部署。

Method: 作者针对实际可实现的次优编码方案推导平均比特翻转次数的闭式表达式，用于性能评估；同时设计两种由预定义随机码本辅助的新编码方案，在降低计算复杂度的同时尽量逼近最优编码的翻转减少效果。

Result: 实验/分析表明，低复杂度次优方案的性能非常接近复杂最优方案。例如，对64位数据增加8个冗余位时，次优方案可实现约24.7%的比特翻转减少，而最优方案约为26.4%。

Conclusion: 实用次优编码能够以显著更低的复杂度获得接近最优的比特翻转减少效果，因此适合用于低功耗总线、非易失存储器等对能耗和寿命敏感的系统。

Abstract: We consider the transmission of data encoded into binary messages, with the goal of minimizing the Hamming distance, i.e., the number of bit-flips, between consecutive messages. This problem is relevant for enhancing the longevity of Non-Volatile Memories and reducing transition-induced energy consumption in data buses. Known as Write-Efficient Memory coding in the literature, this challenge has traditionally been addressed using optimal but complex schemes. In low-power computer systems the same topic is known as bus encoding. In this paper, we derive closed-form expressions to evaluate the average number of bit-flips for practical, sub-optimal encoding schemes, and propose two new schemes assisted by predefined random codebooks. We demonstrate that low-complexity solutions achieve performance very close to the optimal schemes, making them attractive for implementation in energy-sensitive and memory-critical applications. For instance, by adding 8 extra bits to 64-bits data, sub-optimal schemes can achieve a bit-flip reduction (related to energy saving) of approximately 24.7%, compared to the 26.4% reduction offered by the significantly more complex optimal scheme.

</details>


### [158] [Block Tensor Rank of Sum-Rank Metric Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14205)
*Huimin Lao,Huy Pham,Hoang Ta,Van Khu Vu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为求和秩度量码引入块张量秩不变量，并证明其可按块加性分解，从而给出多种下界及可达构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求和秩码统一了汉明度量码与秩度量码，但其结构复杂；作者希望用一种新的不变量——块张量秩——刻画代码在块内秩一简单张量生成意义下的复杂度，并降低其计算难度。

Method: 定义块张量秩为包含该码的最小块简单张量线性张成数；证明该秩在各块投影上可加分解；基于块投影与坐标码分别建立下界，并结合经典汉明度量中的Singleton界与Griesmer界得到显式坐标码下界；再利用达到经典界的汉明码构造达到相应下界的求和秩码族。

Result: 得到投影式下界、坐标码下界、Singleton坐标码下界与Griesmer坐标码下界；构造了若干达到Singleton或Griesmer坐标码下界的求和秩码族；同时指出文献中某些已知求和秩码族在特定情况下不能达到Singleton坐标码下界。

Conclusion: 块张量秩为分析求和秩码提供了新的结构性工具，其按块分解性质显著简化了计算，并将求和秩码的复杂度问题与经典汉明码界和张量秩问题联系起来。

Abstract: Sum-rank codes provide a generalized framework for Hamming and rank-metric codes, with codewords represented as tuples of matrices and weight given by the sum of the block ranks. In this paper, we introduce and study a block-tensor-rank invariant for sum-rank metric codes. To each code, we associate its \emph{block tensor rank}: the smallest number of block-simple tensors, namely rank-one matrices supported inside single blocks, whose linear span contains the code. In general, determining the block tensor rank of a sum-rank code is challenging. Our main structural result shows that the block tensor rank decomposes additively across the blocks of the code, thereby reducing its computation to a tensor-rank problem on each block projection. Consequently, we derive two complementary lower bounds on the block tensor rank, referred to as the \emph{projection-wise bound} and the \emph{coordinate-code bound}. Moreover, by combining the coordinate-code bound with the classical Singleton and Griesmer bounds for codes in the Hamming metric, we obtain explicit lower bounds, called the \emph{Singleton coordinate-code bound} and the \emph{Griesmer coordinate-code bound}, respectively. We further construct families of sum-rank codes whose block tensor ranks attain the Singleton or Griesmer coordinate-code bounds. These constructions are based on Hamming-metric codes achieving the corresponding classical bounds. Finally, we show that, in certain cases, the block tensor ranks of two known families of sum-rank codes in the literature do not attain the Singleton coordinate-code bound.

</details>


### [159] [The Algebraic Limits of Polynomial Information Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14225)
*Yuqing Kong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了满足独立性为零与数据处理不等式的多项式依赖度量存在严格限制：非对称字母表情形在较大字母表一侧不存在非零多项式；对称情形中任何候选多项式都必须被联合分布矩阵行列式平方整除，因而次数至少为 2n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互信息满足刻画变量依赖的关键性质，但它关于联合分布是超越函数，无法从有限样本中构造无偏估计。若存在多项式型依赖度量，则可依据其次数设计精确的有限样本无偏估计，并服务于多任务同伴预测机制设计。

Method: 论文从代数角度研究联合分布上的多项式函数，要求其在独立分布上为零，并满足对任一变量后处理时不增加的数据处理不等式。通过分析不同字母表规模下这些约束对多项式结构的影响，推导不可存在性与整除性定理，并将多项式次数下界映射到同伴预测所需任务数下界。

Result: 主要结果包括：当 |X|>|Y| 时，若要求在较大字母表一侧满足 DPI 且在独立性下消失，则不存在非零多项式依赖度量；当 |X|=|Y|=n 时，任何满足条件的多项式都可被 (det U)^2 整除，因此非平凡候选的次数至少为 2n；基于行列式的互信息达到该下界。

Conclusion: 该工作表明多项式依赖度量远比互信息受限：非对称情形基本不可能，对称情形也存在 2n 的最低次数门槛。这直接给出多任务同伴预测机制的有限任务下界，说明在非对称较大字母表侧不存在有限任务机制，而对称情形至少需要 2n 个任务。

Abstract: The paper studies the existence of \emph{polynomial} measures of dependence between two random variables: polynomial functions of the joint distribution that (i) vanish on independence and (ii) cannot increase under post-processing of either variable (the data processing inequality, DPI). Mutual information satisfies both properties but is transcendental in the joint distribution, making it impossible to estimate without bias from finitely many samples. A polynomial alternative would admit an exact finite-sample unbiased estimator, with the polynomial degree controlling the required sample size.
  The main result is negative: in the asymmetric setting where the variables have different alphabet sizes (\(|X| > |Y|\)), no nonzero polynomial can simultaneously satisfy DPI on the larger-alphabet side and vanish on independence. In the symmetric case \(|X| = |Y| = n\), we establish a structural result: every such polynomial is divisible by \((\det U)^2\), where \(U\) denotes the \(n \times n\) joint distribution matrix. Consequently, any nontrivial candidate must have degree at least \(2n\). The determinant-based mutual information attains this lower bound.
  These algebraic results have direct consequences for \emph{multi-task peer prediction}, a mechanism-design problem in which a principal incentivizes honest reports from agents who observe correlated signals but no ground truth. Every such mechanism running on \(\ell\) independent tasks gives rise to a polynomial measure of dependence of degree at most \(\ell\), so our lower bounds on polynomial degree translate directly into lower bounds on the number of tasks: in the asymmetric case no finite-task mechanism exists on the larger-alphabet side at all, while the symmetric case requires at least \(\ell \geq 2n\) tasks.

</details>


### [160] [Noncoherent ISAC over Block-Fading Channels: Asymptotic Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14400)
*Hao Yang,Kai Wan,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究严格非相干条件下 ISAC 系统的基本性能极限与最优信号分布设计，揭示高 SNR 下感知与通信存在冲突，而低 SNR 下一阶意义上二者可完全协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ISAC 分析多依赖完美信道状态信息的相干框架，但实际块衰落 MIMO 场景中收发双方可能均未知信道。论文旨在刻画严格非相干条件下通信速率与感知性能之间的根本权衡，并指导信号分布设计。

Method: 采用 realization-wise 视角分析不同 SNR 区间的非相干性能。在高 SNR 下推导非相干互信息下界，定义 sensing-induced rate loss 衡量感知导向波束成形带来的通信损失，并用投影梯度算法优化空间功率分配。在低 SNR 下，对遍历最小均方误差进行一阶渐近分析。

Result: 高 SNR 下，通信偏好的酉空时调制结构与感知偏好的任务导向空间功率分配之间存在明显冲突；优化空间功率分配可在二者之间折中。低 SNR 下，感知最优策略退化为沿目标响应主特征向量的秩一传输，且不会造成一阶通信损失。

Conclusion: 严格非相干 ISAC 中，高 SNR 区域存在感知与通信权衡，而低 SNR 区域该冲突渐近消失，感知和通信目标可在一阶意义上实现完美对齐。

Abstract: This paper investigates the fundamental limits and optimal signal distribution design for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems operating under strictly noncoherent conditions. Unlike conventional coherent frameworks that rely on perfect channel state information, we consider a block-fading MIMO channel where the channel realizations are unknown to both the transmitter and the receiver. We adopt a realization-wise perspective to characterize the noncoherent performance tradeoff across different signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regimes. In the high-SNR regime, we derive a lower bound for the noncoherent mutual information and define a metric, termed sensing-induced rate loss, to quantify the communication penalty incurred by sensing-oriented beamforming. We then employ a projected gradient algorithm to optimize the spatial power allocation, balancing the conflict between the unitary space-time modulation-based structure for communication and the task-oriented spatial power allocation for sensing. Conversely, in the low-SNR regime, we perform a first-order asymptotic analysis of the ergodic minimum mean squared error (EMMSE). Our theoretical derivation reveals a fundamental synergy: the sensing-optimal strategy collapses to a rank-one transmission along the dominant eigenvector of the target response, which incurs no first-order communication loss in the low-SNR regime. This result demonstrates that the conflicting tradeoff observed at high SNR vanishes asymptotically at low SNR, enabling perfect alignment between sensing and communication objectives.

</details>


### [161] [Generalized Framework for a Fair Comparison of Cellular and Cooperative Massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14448)
*Leonard Paul Schulz,Stefan Schwarz,Gerhard Bauch*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于图的公平比较框架，用于统一评估蜂窝、协作式与无小区大规模 MIMO 架构，并指出相位对齐的分布式协作波束赋形是主要增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模 MIMO 架构比较往往同时混合了天线分布、站点间协作和处理方式等因素，导致不同系统之间难以公平比较。作者希望将关键设计维度解耦，建立统一模型和指标来评估不同架构的真实性能差异。

Method: 论文构建图模型框架，区分“天线分布”和“站点间协作”两个核心属性，定义七类代表性系统；推导兼容的上行和下行频谱效率表达式，包括适用于混合瞬时与统计有效 CSI 检测器的上行界；并将可扩展用户关联和处理规则推广到所有架构。

Result: 大规模数值仿真表明，公平比较需要比常见设置更大的仿真区域，至少为 2.5×2.5 km²。论文提出“相对容量”指标，用于衡量各架构接近集中式无小区处理性能的程度。结果显示，跨空间分布天线的协调相位对齐波束赋形是协作增益的主要来源。

Conclusion: 在密集部署且每个接入点天线数较少的场景中，协作式分布式天线系统和混合无小区架构可以在较弱中传假设下获得接近集中式无小区系统的大部分性能，因此是性能与部署复杂度之间较有吸引力的折中方案。

Abstract: Cooperative massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) promises large gains over cellular deployments, but existing comparisons of different architectures often mix antenna distribution, inter-site coordination, and processing assumptions. This paper introduces a graph-based framework for fair comparison of cellular, coordinated, and cell-free massive-MIMO systems. We differentiate between two key properties, namely antenna distribution and inter-site cooperation, which yields seven representative system types. We derive compatible uplink and downlink spectral efficiency (SE) expressions, including an uplink bound for detectors with mixed instantaneous and statistical effective channel state information (CSI), and adapt scalable user association and processing rules to all considered architectures. We evaluate these systems using extensive numerical simulations and show that for a fair comparison much larger simulation areas (at least 2.5 $\times$ 2.5 km2) than commonly used are required. We introduce the relative capacity, which measures how closely each architecture approaches centralized cell-free processing. The results show that coordinated, phase-aligned beamforming across spatially distributed antennas is the main source of cooperation gains. In dense deployments with few antennas per access point (AP), coordinated Distributed Antenna System (DAS) and hybrid cell-free architectures achieve much of the centralized cell-free performance while requiring substantially weaker midhaul assumptions.

</details>


### [162] [Nonlinear Two-Time-Scale Stochastic Approximation: A Sharp Phase Transition and How to Beat It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14488)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了非线性双时间尺度随机逼近中慢变量均方收敛率的正则性相变边界：未校正更新的速率为 O(k^{-1}+k^{-a(1+ρ)})，仅当 a(1+ρ)≥1 时可保证解耦的 k^{-1} 速率；通过引入在线偏差估计并校正慢更新，可在所有 ρ∈[0,1] 下恢复 O(k^{-1})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有有限时间分析表明，在收缩性假设下，非线性双时间尺度随机逼近的慢迭代通常只能达到 k^{-a} 量级均方速率，而解耦的 k^{-1} 速率通常需要强局部线性条件。本文旨在明确非线性余项正则性如何决定慢变量速率边界，并判断较慢速率是算法本身缺陷还是不可避免的信息论障碍。

Method: 作者构造速率决定的 normal form：慢漂移包含局部线性泄漏项和阶数为 1+ρ 的非线性余项。先对未校正递推建立非渐近上界，并用标量高斯下界证明该速率匹配且不可避免。随后引入辅助在线偏差估计器 M_{k+1}=M_k+γ_k(R(X_k)-M_k)，其中 β_k≪γ_k≪α_k，并在慢更新中减去 M_k。最后通过局部化转移定理将 normal form 中的相变机制推广到一般非线性 TTSA 的快流形坐标中，证明依赖两个 Abel 变换抵消。

Result: 未校正递推满足 E||Y_k||^2 ≤ C(k^{-1}+k^{-a(1+ρ)})，且存在匹配的标量高斯下界，说明当 a(1+ρ)<1 时较慢项不可避免。解耦 k^{-1} 速率对未校正递推而言当且仅当 a(1+ρ)≥1。引入在线偏差估计并校正后，在相同稳定性、矩条件与余项假设下，校正递推对所有 ρ∈[0,1] 均达到 E||Ỹ_k||^2=O(k^{-1})。

Conclusion: 论文给出了双时间尺度随机逼近慢变量收敛率的尖锐正则性依赖边界，并表明未校正算法中的慢速率并非信息论不可突破，而是可通过在线偏差追踪与校正消除。其理论贡献在于同时提供匹配上下界、相变条件和向一般非线性 TTSA 的局部转移框架。

Abstract: Recent finite-time analyses of nonlinear two-time-scale stochastic approximation show that under contractive assumptions the slow iterate $Y_k$ with stepsizes $β_k=Θ(k^{-1})$ and $α_k=Θ(k^{-a})$, $a\in(1/2,1)$, generally satisfies a mean-square rate of order $k^{-a}$; decoupled $k^{-1}$ rates require strong local linearity. We identify a sharp regularity-dependent boundary. In a rate-determining normal form where the slow drift contains a locally linear leakage and a nonlinear remainder of order $1+ρ$ ($ρ\in[0,1]$), the uncorrected recursion satisfies \[ \mathbb{E}\|Y_k\|^2 \le C\bigl(k^{-1}+k^{-a(1+ρ)}\bigr), \] and a matching scalar Gaussian lower bound shows that the slower term is unavoidable without modifying the update. Thus the decoupled $k^{-1}$ rate is guaranteed for the uncorrected recursion exactly when $a(1+ρ)\ge 1$. This lower bound concerns only the naive update; it is not an information-theoretic obstruction. We demonstrate this by equipping the normal-form recursion with an auxiliary online bias estimator \[ M_{k+1}=M_k+γ_k(R(X_k)-M_k),\qquad β_k\llγ_k\llα_k, \] and subtracting $M_k$ from the slow update. Under the same stability, moment, and remainder assumptions, the corrected recursion achieves $\mathbb{E}\|\widetilde Y_k\|^2=O(k^{-1})$ for every $ρ\in[0,1]$, including regimes where the uncorrected update provably suffers the slower rate. Finally, we prove localized transfer theorems that extend the phase-transition mechanism to general nonlinear TTSA in fast-manifold coordinates. The proofs are non-asymptotic and rely on two Abel-transform cancellations: one for the locally linear fast-error leakage, and one for the tracked nonlinear bias.

</details>


### [163] [Asymptotically Optimal Codes for Correcting Burst Deletions and Insertions in Labeled DNA Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14573)
*Wenhao Liu,Zhengyi Jiang,Zhongyi Huang,Hanxu Hou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究DNA数据存储中荧光标签序列的突发同步错误纠正问题，提出突发 t 删除/插入 A-标签码，给出信息论下界、显式构造与渐近最优冗余结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光标记是DNA可视化与DNA数据存储随机访问的重要机制，但合成、杂交和光学读出等生化过程具有随机性，会在标签序列中引入突发删除或插入等同步错误。现有编码理论尚缺乏针对标签域突发同步错误的严格界限和高效构造。

Method: 作者形式化定义 burst t-deletion/insertion A-labeling codes，用于纠正标签域中单个长度为 t 的突发删除或插入；建立任意此类码的冗余信息论下界；针对 t≥2、t|n 且 n≥7t+3 的情形，设计 O(n^2) 时间的显式编码和解码算法，并引入广义游程长度受限约束以协调DNA编码域与标签错误域之间的结构差异。

Result: 论文证明任意此类编码在 t≥1 且 t|n 时冗余至少为 log_4 n + O(1)；提出的构造达到 log_4 n + (t-1)log_4 log_{8/3} n + O(1) 的冗余，并支持 O(n^2) 时间编码/解码。

Conclusion: 该工作为DNA标签序列突发同步错误纠正建立了首个基础性信息论下界，并给出接近最优的显式构造；对于固定 t，其冗余主导项与下界匹配，仅有 O(log log n) 额外开销，因而具有渐近最优性。

Abstract: Fluorescent labeling is a cornerstone of DNA visualization and a key enabler of random access in DNA-based data storage. However, the stochastic nature of biochemical processes, including synthesis, hybridization, and optical readout, induces \emph{burst} synchronization errors within the resulting labeling sequences. To address this critical challenge, we formally introduce \emph{burst $t$-deletion/insertion $\mathcal{A}$-labeling codes,} designed to correct a single burst of $t$ deletions or insertions in the label domain. Our contributions are threefold.
  \begin{itemize}
  \item \textbf{Fundamental limit.} We establish an information-theoretic lower bound of $\log_4 n + \mathcal{O}(1)$ on the redundancy of any such code for all $t \ge 1$ with $t \mid n$. To the best of our knowledge, this resolves the first information-theoretic lower bound even for the single-error case \(t=1\).
  \item \textbf{Explicit construction.} For $t \ge 2$, $t \mid n$, and $n \ge 7t + 3$, we propose explicit encoding and decoding algorithms, both running in $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ time. A novel generalized Run-Length Limited (RLL) constraint is introduced to bridge the structural mismatch between the DNA encoding domain and the label error domain.
  \item \textbf{Asymptotic optimality.} The proposed scheme achieves redundancy $\log_4 n + (t-1)\log_4 \log_{8/3} n + \mathcal{O}(1)$, matching the dominant term of the lower bound up to a small $\mathcal{O}(\log\log n)$ overhead, rendering the construction asymptotically optimal for fixed $t$.
  \end{itemize}

</details>
